Let's formulate resizing nonnegative sequence:
```
def resize_seq(a, m):
    # cumulative x = arange(n), y=cumsum(a)/sum, x2=arange(m), interpolate cumulative, diff
    S=a.sum(); if S<=0: ones; x=np.linspace(0,n-1,n); y=cumsum/S; xnew=np.linspace(0,m-1,m); ynew=np.interp(xnew,x,y); out=np.diff([0,ynew]); out*=S? We only care shape; return out*sqrt? We'll normalize later.
```
This preserves total mass and shape. Interpolation can smooth. Use monotonic. Good.

Optimization algorithm details:
Use exact evaluation via evaluate_sequence? We can define compute_score(a) returning exact 2n max c / S². For p scaled a=sqrt(n)p, compute score = 2*max(c)/? Since S=sqrt(n), score = 2n*max(c)/n=2 max(c). For arbitrary a, use general.

Implement `lse_score_grad_z(z, beta, n, Lc, Lh, sqrt_n)`:
- z = z - z.mean()
- zm = z - max(z) (for stability)
- p = np.exp(zm); p /= p.sum()
- a = sqrt_n * p
- A = np.fft.rfft(a, Lc)
- c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, Lc)[:2n-1]
- M = c.max()
- y = beta * (c - M); clip [-700, 700]
- e = np.exp(y); e_sum = e.sum()
- if e_sum too small: q = one-hot max; L = M
- else L = M + np.log(e_sum)/beta; q = e/e_sum
- h = conv q with a_rev length 3n-2:
   * q is length 2n-1. Need FFT length Lh. P = rfft(q, Lh); RA = rfft(a[::-1], Lh); h=irfft(P*RA, Lh)[n-1:2n-1]
- Gp = 4.0 * sqrt_n * (h - L)
- C = dot(p, Gp)
- gz = p * (Gp - C)
- scale_output = maybe 1.0; score = 2.0 * L  (because sum fixed sqrt_n)
Return score, gz.

Let's consider the "LP update" more. It might be a globally convergent method for this minimization. We can approximate it using projected gradient but perhaps we can implement a faster dual via SOR. At current a (call f), M=c_max. Solve LP max 1^T g s.t. F g ≤ M 1, g≥0. This can be seen as finding maximum weight independent? Since F has intervals, but weights arbitrary f. We can solve it in O(n log n) using specialized algorithm? Matrix F_{k,i}=f_{k-i} for k-i∈[0,n-1]. For each column i, variable g_i appears in rows k=i..i+n-1 with weights f_{k-i}. The constraints are sliding window sums of g weighted by f. This is a system of difference constraints in 1D. The LP:
max ∑ g_i
s.t. ∑_{j=i?} f_{j} g_{k-j} ≤ M for k=0..2n-2, g_i≥0.
This is a linear program with a convex (band) constraint: for all k, convolution (f,g)_k ≤M. Since f,g nonnegative, this is like cap on weighted moving sum. Gradient/feasible path.

For a fixed f, to maximize sum g, this is a standard "maximum weight with sliding window capacity". We can solve using linear programming dual, but maybe has greedy solution? Let's inspect dual:
Primal max 1^T g
A g ≤ M 1_A
g ≥0
Dual min M 1_B^T y
A^T y ≥ 1_B
y ≥0
A_{k,i}=f_{k-i}. A^T y_i = ∑_{k} f_{k-i} y_k = (y*f)_i? Similar. Choose y_k nonnegative such that weighted convolution y*f at least 1 for all i, minimize sum y_k. Then g_i>0 if equality. This resembles finding minimal y such that f*y ≥1. For binomial f, maybe y_i=1 works? f*L/2? Because f*f lower=1. If y all? The minimal sum dual maybe gives LP value.

Could solve dual via iterative multiplication: Given f, find y≥0 with f*y ≥1 minimizing sum y. Since f>0, the minimal y in componentwise sense is y_i =1/(f_i?)? But convolution couples. This is like covering. Maybe can approximate by y_i = max(0, 1 - (f*y excluding i)_i)/f_i using SOR. Could be faster than LP.

Then primal g_i maybe M / (y? no). At optimal, active set. But maybe simpler: use a coordinate ascent: for each i, given other g, max g_i subject constraints: for all k in support, g_i ≤ (M - sum_{j≠i} f_{k-j}g_j)/f_{k-i}. So g_i = min over k of remaining / f_{k-i}. This upper depends on others; iterating g_i = max(0, min_k ... ). This is an asynchronous Gauss-Seidel for the LP. We can implement? For n=7000, each variable min over n rows -> O(n²) too slow. But maybe use FFT to compute remaining sums? The vector of constraint margins R_k = M - (f*g)_k. Detailed function for LSE gradient in z for given n with precomputed lengths:
```
def _compute_lse_grad_z(z, beta, n, Lc, Lh, sqrt_n):
    z = z - z.mean()
    p = soft(z)
    a = sqrt_n * p
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, Lc)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, Lc)[:2*n-1]
    M = float(c.max())
    y = np.clip(beta*(c-M), -700, 700)
    e = np.exp(y)
    es = e.sum()
    if es < 1e-300: q = ...
    else: q=e/es; L=M+log(es)/beta
    # h
    P = np.fft.rfft(q, Lh)
    RA = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], Lh)
    h = np.fft.irfft(RA*P, Lh)[n-1:2*n-1]
    Gp = 4.0*sqrt_n*(h - L)
    C = float(np.dot(p, Gp))
    gz = p*(Gp - C)
    score = 2.0*L
    return score, gz
```

```
def adam_optimize(z, n, beta_schedule, time_budget, max_steps_per_beta=None):
    Lc=next_pow2(2n-1); Lh=next_pow2(3n-2); sqrt_n=np.sqrt(n)
    t=0;m=zeros;nada? v=zeros; step=1; best_z=z; best_exact=np.inf
    def exact_from_z(z): p=softmax; a=sqrt_n*p; return compute_score_a(a)
    best_exact=exact_from_z(z)
    for beta in beta_schedule:
       max_time = ... maybe allocate by beta
       while time < end and steps<...:
           sc_lse, g = _compute_lse_grad_z(z,beta,...)
           # if lse not finite revert
           m = b1*m + (1-b1)*g
           vhat...
           z = z - lr * mhat/(sqrt(v)+eps)
           z = z - mean(z)
           # optional if exact every 50
           if step%check==0:
              sc=exact_from_z(z)
              if sc<best_exact: best...
              if sc > best_exact*1.5 + 0.3: # diverge, reduce lr or revert
                 z=best_z.copy(); m*=0.5; v*=0.5; lr*=0.5?
    return best_z,best_exact
```

Top-level global config for scipy:
```
CFG = {'n':0,'Lc':0,'Lh':0,'sqrt_n':0,'beta':1.0,'scale':1.0}
def lse_z_obj(x):
   x=np.asarray(x)
   sc,g=_lse_grad_z(x, CFG['beta'], CFG['n'], CFG['Lc'], CFG['Lh'], CFG['sqrt_n'])
   return sc*CFG['scale']
def lse_z_obj_grad(x):
   sc,g=_lse_grad_z(...)
   return sc*CFG['scale'], g*CFG['scale']
```

Initial binomial function for n:
```
def binomial_sqrt_seq(n):
    b=np.empty(n); b[0]=1.0
    for i in range(n-1): b[i+1]=b[i]*(2*i+1)/(2*i+2)
    return b
```
Order: i from 0 to n-2: b_{i+1}=b_i*(2i+1)/(2i+2). For b1=.5, b2=.375. Good. For n=10000, values down to .005. Sum ~112? sqrt(n)=100, close. Score ~? Good.

Resizing function:
```
def resize_seq_to_len(a, m):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<=0 or a.size==0: return np.ones(m)/m
    x=np.linspace(0, a.size-1, a.size)
    y=np.cumsum(a); y/=y[-1]
    xnew=np.linspace(0, m-1, m)
    ynew=np.interp(xnew, x, y)
    out=np.diff(np.r_[0.0, ynew])
    # remove small negatives from interpolation
    out=np.clip(out,0,None)
    if out.sum()<1e-12: out=np.ones(m)/m
    return out * np.sqrt(m)  # or return raw shape; we'll normalize.
```
Return raw nonneg mass sum maybe S? We can normalize to sum√m later. For initial, can just return p=out.

From sequence to z:
```
def seq_to_z(a, mode=None):
    a=np.clip(np.asarray(a),0,None)
    if a.sum()<1e-12: a=np.ones(len(a))
    p=a/a.sum()
    p=np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    z=np.log(p)
    z -= z.mean()
    return z
```

def adam_optimize_z(z, n, Lc, Lh, sqrt_n, beta, time_limit, lr=0.05, check_freq=100, label=''):
    start=time
    m=np.zeros_like(z); v=np.zeros_like(z); t=1
    b1=0.9; b2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    best_z=z.copy(); best_exact=exact_score_from_z(z...)
    n_check=0
    while time-start < time_limit:
       sc,lse_grad = smooth_z_score_grad(...)
       if not finite: z=best_z; m*=0; v*=0; continue
       m=b1*m+(1-b1)*g
       v=b2*v+(1-b2)*g*g
       mhat=m/(1-b1**t); vhat=v/(1-b2**t)
       z = z - lr*mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps)
       z -= z.mean()
       t+=1
       if t%check_freq==0:
          sc_exact=exact_from_z(z)
          if sc_exact<best_exact: best...
          # adapt lr: if exact increased more than best? Use if exact > best_exact + 0.01 and t>check_freq*5: revert? maybe reduce lr.
          if sc_exact > best_exact*1.1? # score 1.5 -> 1.65, too high. Use threshold 0.05.
              if sc_exact > best_exact + 0.05:
                  z=best_z.copy(); m=m*0.2; v=v*0.5; lr*=0.7
    return best_z, best_exact
```

```
def gradient_line_search(a, n, Lc, time_limit, num_peaks=3, beta=10000, max_evals=20):
   best=a; best_sc=exact_score(a)
   while time < limit:
      c = conv(a,Lc)
      M=c.max(); S=a.sum(); if S<... break
      # weights q: top peaks weighted by exp((c-M)*beta/ M?) Since c values O(1), use beta maybe 50
      if num_peaks>=len(c): q uniform
      vals = c - M
      weights = np.exp(np.clip((vals - M?)? Actually M is max so max 0. Use vals = beta*(c-M) (≤0). small for nonpeaks. For top peaks, can set q = normalized over top K with equal or weight.
      # simpler: idx=top K ascending; q= ones at idx / K
      h = conv(q, a_rev) (different Lh)
      grad = 2*n*(h/S² - 2*M a/S³)
      d = -grad; d -= mean(d)
      # line search d
      a_new, sc = line_search_a(a, d, Lc, max_evals)
      if sc<best_sc: best=a_new; a=a_new
      else: # try random direction? or break if no imp
         break? For exact gradient, if no improvement, maybe local. Try a perturbed gradient/random before continue.
```

`linconv(a, L)`:
```
def linconv(a, L):
    n=len(a)
    return np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(a, L)*np.fft.rfft(a, L), L)[:2*n-1]
```
This computes A twice; better:
```
A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); return np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:2*n-1]
```

`exact_score(a, L=None)`:
```
def exact_score(a, L=None):
  a=np.asarray(a,float); n=len(a); S=float(a.sum()); if S<1e-12: return inf
  if L is None: L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
  c=linconv(a, L); M=float(np.max(c)); return 2*n*M/(S*S)
```

`cross_h(q, a, Lh)`:
```
def correlation_h(q, a, Lh):
  n=len(a)
  RA=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], Lh); PQ=np.fft.rfft(q, Lh); return np.fft.irfft(RA*PQ, Lh)[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Need q length 2n-1. If Lh too small? next_pow2(len(q)+n-1)=3n-2.

`smooth_z_score_grad(z, beta, n, Lc, Lh, sqrt_n)`:
```

z0 = z - np.max(z)

A=np.fft.rfft(a,Lc); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,Lc)[:2*n-1]
# clip c small negative? no for max but q weights. c = np.maximum(c,0) maybe to avoid exp positive? If tiny negative, max unaffected. Set c = np.where(c>0,c,0)
M=float(np.max(c))

e=np.exp(y); es=e.sum()
if es<1e-300:
   q=np.zeros(2*n-1); q[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; L=M
else:
   q=e/es; L=M+np.log(es)/beta
h=correlation_h(q,a,Lh)
# h may negative tiny; clip 0
h = np.where(h>0, h, 0.0)
Gp = 4.0 * sqrt_n * h
C = float(np.dot(p, Gp))
gz = p*(Gp - C)
# optional if gz has nan
lse_score=2.0*L
exact_score_val=2.0*M
return lse_score, exact_score_val, gz, c
```

```
def make_lag_weights(c, num_top, beta):
    m=len(c)
    q=np.zeros(m)
    M=c.max()
    # if beta is None or inf: top num_top equal? 
    if beta is None or beta > 1e12:
       k=min(num_top,m)
       idx=np.argpartition(c, m-k)[-k:]
       q[idx]=1.0/k
    else:
       y=np.clip(beta*(c-M), -700,700)
       e=np.exp(y); s=e.sum()
       if s>0: q=e/s
       # optionally restrict to top num_top? LSE over all gives weights concentrated on high peaks. Fine.
    return q
```

def peak_direction_h(a, q, Lh):

Line search function with exact eval. Need pass Lc. Use base_sum maybe. Let's implement exactly:
```
def line_search_a(base, d, n, Lc, max_evals=24, verbose=False):
    a0=np.asarray(base,float); d=np.asarray(d,float)
    d = d - np.mean(d)  # ensure sum zero
    dnrm=float(np.linalg.norm(d))
    if dnrm<1e-15: return a0, exact_score_fast(a0,n,Lc)
    max_base=max(float(np.max(a0)),1e-12)
    d = d / (max_base*10.0)  # so max change at λ=1 is 0.1 max_base
    # feasible bounds:
    lo = -np.inf; hi = np.inf
    pos = d > 1e-15
    if pos.any(): lo = max(lo, float(np.max(-a0[pos]/d[pos])))
    neg = d < -1e-15
    if neg.any(): hi = min(hi, float(np.max(a0[neg]/(-d[neg]))))
    # Also impose reasonable range [-5,5]
    lo = max(lo, -5.0)
    hi = min(hi, 5.0)
    if not (lo < 0.0 < hi):
        # Try adjust: maybe only one sign feasible; include 0 and boundary
        if lo >= hi:
           # use small fixed interval
           step=0.05 if dnrm>0 else 0
           lo=min(0.0, -step); hi=max(0.0, step)
        else:
           pass
    if hi - lo < 1e-8:
        return a0, exact_score_fast(a0,n,Lc)
    best_lam=0.0; best_sc=exact_score_fast(a0,n,Lc)
    # Evaluate 0 already
    # Golden over [lo,hi]
    gr=(np.sqrt(5)-1)/2; if_gr=1-gr
    a=lo; b=hi
    # To not miss 0 if not similar, golden will sample.
    c=b-if_gr*(b-a); d2=a+if_gr*(b-a)
    fc=eval(c); fd=eval(d2)
    for i in range(max_evals-2):
       if fc < fd:
           b=d2; d2=c; fd=fc; c=b-if_gr*(b-a); fc=eval(c)
       else:
           a=c; c=d2; fc=fd; d2=a+if_gr*(b-a); fd=eval(d2)
       if abs(b-a) < 1e-8*max(1,abs(lam?)) break
    mid=(a+b)/2; fm=eval(mid)
    # choose best among current evaluations? Keep fc/fd/fm.
```

Now, memory/time. `binomial_sqrt` loop O(n) in Python might be slow for 10000? 10000 loop okay. Use vectorized recurrence? Recurrence with `np.cumprod`:
```
def binomial_sqrt(n):
   if n==1: return np.array([1.])
   k=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
   # b_k = prod_{j=1}^k (2j-1)/(2j)
   num = 2*k[1:]-1; den=2*k[1:]
   out=np.empty(n); out[0]=1.0; out[1:]=np.cumprod(num/den); return out
```
This works: for k=1 ratio1/2, k=2 ratio3/4 => .375, good. Use float.

Random direction lowpass:
```
def random_lowpass_direction(rng,n,band=0.5):
  L=1 << (n-1).bit_length()
  Lh=L//2+1
  freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(L)
  filter=1.0/(1.0+(freqs*max(1,band*2))**2) # maybe
  F=(rng.standard_normal(Lh)+1j*rng.standard_normal(Lh))*filter
  F[0]=0.0
  d=np.fft.irfft(F,L)[:n]
  d-=np.mean(d)
  return d
```
If n large, using `band` maybe `band=n/10`? freqs range 0..0.5 cycles/sample. To make smooth over n, filter low frequencies: `fc = 0.05` cycles/sample? Then `filter=1/(1+(freqs/fc)^2)`. Use fc=1/(n/10) ~10/n. For n=7000, fc=0.0014 cycles/sample. This yields very smooth (wavelength ~800). Good. But random directions too smooth may not escape; use band random [1/(20), 1/2]? Could. Use `fc = rng.uniform(1.0/(5*n), 1.0/(5.0))`? Actually cycles/sample: max 0.5. fc from 1/(20n) to 1/2. Good.

Detailed functions code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

CFG = {...}

def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << int((m-1).bit_length())

def linconv(a, L):
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:2*len(a)-1]
```
If a length 0? no.

`exact_score_fast(a, n, Lc)`:
```
def exact_score_fast(a, n, Lc):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12: return float('inf')
    c = linconv(a, Lc)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if M < 0: M=0
    return float(2.0*n*M/(S*S))
```
For general exact_score without L:
```
def exact_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(...); n=len(a); S=sum; Lc=next_pow2(2*n-1); return exact_score_fast(a,n,Lc)
```

`correlation_h(q,a,Lh)` as above.

`make_q(c, mode, beta)`:
```
def make_q(c, mode, beta):
    m=len(c); q=np.zeros(m)
    M=float(np.max(c))
    if mode == 'top1':
       q[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
    elif mode == 'top5':
       k=min(5,m); idx=np.argpartition(c, m-k)[-k:]; q[idx]=1.0/k
    elif mode == 'top10':
       k=min(10,m); idx=np.argpartition(c, m-k)[-k:]; q[idx]=1.0/k
    else:
       b = max(1.0, beta)
       y=np.clip(b*(c-M), -700.0, 700.0)
       e=np.exp(y); s=float(e.sum())
       if s>1e-300: q=e/s
       else: q[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
    return q
```
Note `argpartition` with m-k; if k=m, m-k=0 okay? `np.argpartition(c, 0)[-m:]` works if k=m? k=min(5,m). If m<5, k=m. If k=m, m-k=0. Good.

def line_search_a(base, d, n, Lc, max_evals=24):
    base=np.asarray(base,float); d=np.asarray(d,float).copy()
    dsum=float(d.sum()); d -= dsum/n  # sum zero
    dnrm=float(np.linalg.norm(d))
    if dnrm < 1e-14:
       sc=exact_score_fast(base,n,Lc); return base.copy(), sc
    max_base = max(float(np.max(base)), 1e-12)

    def eval_lam(lam): # but no closure allowed? Inside function, nested? Rule says no closures from function nesting. Ugh. `random_direction(rng,n,kind)`:
```
def random_direction(rng, n, kind=0):
  if kind==0: # lowpass Fourier
     L=1 << (n-1).bit_length()
     Lh=L//2+1
     freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(L)
     fc = rng.uniform(1.0/(20.0*n), 0.25)
     filt = 1.0/(1.0+(freqs/fc + 1e-15)**2)
     F = (rng.standard_normal(Lh)+1j*rng.standard_normal(Lh))*filt
     F[0]=0.0
     d=np.fft.irfft(F, L)[:n]
  elif kind==1:
     # block
     d=np.zeros(n)
     k=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//100), max(2,n//20)))
     i=int(rng.integers(0, n-k+1)); j=int(rng.integers(0,n-k+1))
     d[i:i+k]=1.0; d[j:j+k]=d[j:j+k]-2.0? Need ensure sum zero. Simpler: pick two intervals, add + on one, - on other, then mean.
     d[i:i+k]=1.0
     if j!=i: d[j:j+k]=-1.0
     d-=np.mean(d)
  else:
     # coordinate random
     d=np.zeros(n)
     cnt=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//100), max(5,n//50)))
     inc=rng.integers(0,n,cnt); dec=rng.integers(0,n,cnt)
     d[inc]+=1.0; d[dec]-=1.0
     d-=np.mean(d)
  return d
```
For block, if intervals overlap, sum not zero but mean subtract. Good.

`local_search` code top-level:
```
def local_search(a, n, Lc, Lh, time_limit, rng, label=''):
    start=time.monotonic()
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score_fast(a,n,Lc)
    it=0; no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic()-start < time_limit:
        it +=1
        c=linconv(a,Lc)
        M=float(np.max(c)); S=float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(M) or S<1e-12:
            a=best_a; 
            if no_improve>4: break
            continue
        mode = it % 4
        if mode==0: q=make_q(c,'top1',0)
        elif mode==1: q=make_q(c,'top10',0)
        elif mode==2: q=make_q(c,'lse',20.0)
        else: q=make_q(c,'lse',200.0)
        h=correlation_h(q,a,Lh)
        h=np.where(h>0,h,0)
        d=-h; d-=np.mean(d)
        sc_new, a_try = line_search_a(a,d,n,Lc,max_evals=18)
        if sc_new < best_sc - 1e-10:
            best_sc=sc_new; best_a=a_try.copy(); a=a_try.copy(); no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            # try random if no_improve even or always with small chance
            if no_improve % 2 == 0:
                kind=(no_improve//2) % 3
                dr=random_direction(rng,n,kind)
                sc_rand, a_rand = line_search_a(a,dr,n,Lc,max_evals=14)
                if sc_rand < best_sc - 1e-10:
                   best_sc=sc_rand; best_a=a_rand.copy(); a=a_rand.copy(); no_improve=0
                else:
                   if no_improve > 40:
                      break
    # final if best_sc > exact of a? a maybe current not best; return best
    if time.monotonic()-start < time_limit*0.5:
       # maybe no iterations? return
    return best_a, best_sc
```
This uses exact best, not tracking current if current worse; a remains last incrementally accepted? If grad direction no improvement but random maybe, a unchanged. Good.
Potential issue: local_search accepts only if `sc_new < best_sc`, not if current a is not best? a always best after accepts. Good.

Time in local_search: `line_search_a` uses many FFTs; if no_improve and random, double. But okay. Use no_improve > 20 maybe break to save. Set 30.

`adam_optimize_z` top-level:

def adam_optimize_z(z, n, Lc, Lh, sqrt_n, time_limit, beta_schedule=None):
    if beta_schedule is None: beta_schedule=[...]
    start=time.monotonic(); z=np.array(z,float); best_z=z.copy(); best_exact=inf
    m=np.zeros_like(z); v=np.zeros_like(z); t=1; b1=0.9; b2=0.99; eps=1e-8
    # total time fractions
    total_fr=sum(f for _,f in beta_schedule)
    for beta, frac in beta_schedule:
       t_end = start + time_limit*(frac/total_fr) # shouldn't use start? Better use time.monotonic? Use allocated_abs = start + time_limit*frac/total, but if previous took longer? Use global cumulative.
```
Better allocate sequential time:
```
    remaining=time_limit
    for index,(beta,frac) in enumerate(beta_schedule):
        if time.monotonic()-start >= time_limit: break
        if index == len(beta_schedule)-1: sub=time_limit-(time.monotonic()-start)
        else: sub=min(time_limit - (time.monotonic()-start), time_limit*frac/total_fr)
        lr=...
        stall=0
        t_beta_start=time.monotonic()
        while time.monotonic()-t_beta_start < sub and time.monotonic()-start < time_limit:
            lse_sc, exact_sc, g, c = smooth_z_score_grad(...)
            if not finite: z=best_z; m*=0; v*=0; lr*=0.5; continue
            if exact_sc < best_exact:
                best_exact=exact_sc; best_z=z.copy(); stall=0
            else:
                stall += 1
                if stall > 3000:
                    # reduce lr or reset
                    if exact_sc > best_exact + 0.02:
                        z=best_z.copy(); m=m*0.2; v=v*0.5; lr*=0.7; stall=0
                    else:
                        lr*=0.85; stall=0
            m=...; v=...
            z -= lr * mhat/(sqrt(v)+eps)
            z -= z.mean()
            # optional clip range
            zmax=z.max(); zmin=z.min(); if zmax-zmin>300: z = z - zmin - 150.0; z-=z.mean()
            t +=1
        # at end beta, if current exact >> best reset?
        lse_sc, exact_sc, g, c = smooth...
        if not finite: z=best_z
        elif exact_sc > best_exact + 0.05:
             z=best_z.copy()
    # final current exact
    lse_sc, exact_sc, g, c = smooth...
    current_z=z; current_exact=exact_sc
    if current_exact < best_exact: best...
    return best_z, best_exact, current_z, current_exact
```

`z_to_a(z,sqrt_n)` top-level:
```
def softmax_z(z):
   z = np.asarray(z,float); z=z-z.mean(); z0=z-z.max(); p=np.exp(z0); p=p/p.sum(); return p
def z_to_a(z, sqrt_n): return sqrt_n*softmax_z(z)
```

Check `binomial_sqrt`: If n=0? We won't call n 0. Ensure n int.
```
def binomial_sqrt(n):
    n=int(n)
    if n<=0: return np.array([])
    out=np.empty(n,dtype=float)
    out[0]=1.0
    if n>1:
        k=np.arange(1,n,dtype=float)
        out[1:]=np.cumprod((2.0*k-1.0)/(2.0*k))
    return out
```
For k=1:1/2. Good.

`resize_seq_to_len`: If a size 1, interpolation? `np.linspace(0,0,1)`, `np.interp` okay. Use `a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0)`. If sum<1e-12 return ones. Return raw length m.
```
def resize_seq_to_len(a, m):
    m=int(m)
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0); a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-12 or a.size==0: return np.ones(m,dtype=float)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,a.size-1.0,a.size)
    y=np.cumsum(a); y/y[-1]
    xnew=np.linspace(0.0,m-1.0,m)
    ynew=np.interp(xnew,x,y)
    out=np.diff(np.r_[0.0,ynew])
    out=np.clip(out,0.0,None)
    if out.sum()<1e-12: out=np.ones(m)*S/m
    out *= (S/out.sum())  # preserve total maybe
    return out
```
If m=0? no.

`scale_to_sqrt`:
```
def scale_to_sqrt(a, n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0); a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if a.size != n: a=resize_seq_to_len(a,n) ; S=...
    if S<1e-12: return np.ones(n)*np.sqrt(n)/n
    return a * (np.sqrt(n)/S)
```

`softmax_z`:
```
def softmax_z(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    z=z-np.mean(z)
    z=z-z.max()
    p=np.exp(z)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<1e-300:
       p=np.ones_like(z)/z.size
    else: p/=s
    return p
```
If z contains nan? assume finite. If not, maybe set zeros.

`seq_to_z`:
```
def seq_to_z(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); a=np.clip(np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0),0,None)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-12: a=np.ones_like(a)
    p=a/S; p=np.maximum(p,1e-300); p=p/p.sum()
    z=np.log(p); z-=z.mean(); return z
```

`z_to_a`:
```
def z_to_a(z, sqrt_n): return sqrt_n*softmax_z(z)
```

```
def z_score(z,n,Lc,sqrt_n):
    p=softmax_z(z); a=sqrt_n*p; return exact_score_fast(a,n,Lc)
```

Now, `lbfgs_polish`: It needs `Lh` for smooth function. It doesn't use `time_limit` inside except maxfun. It returns score final. If minimize result x array maybe list. Use `np.asarray(res.x,float)`. If `res.success` false but x finite.

```
def lbfgs_polish(z, n, Lc, Lh, sqrt_n, beta, time_limit):
    z=np.asarray(z,float).copy(); z=z-np.mean(z)
    sc0=z_score(z,n,Lc,sqrt_n)
    best_z=z; best_sc=sc0
    CFG['n']=n; CFG['Lc']=Lc; CFG['Lh']=Lh; CFG['sqrt_n']=sqrt_n; CFG['beta']=beta; CFG['scale']=max(1.0, n/10.0)
    if time_limit <=0: return best_z, best_sc
    maxfun=int(min(5000, max(200, time_limit*10.0)))
    maxiter=int(max(100, maxfun//5))
    try:
       res=minimize(_lbfgs_fun_grad, z, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':20})
       z_new=np.asarray(res.x,float)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(z_new)): z_new=z
       else: z_new -= np.mean(z_new)
       sc_new=z_score(z_new,n,Lc,sqrt_n)
       if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc:
          best_z=z_new; best_sc=sc_new
       # Also evaluate original if res not
    except Exception:
       pass
    return best_z,best_sc
```
`_lbfgs_fun_grad`:
```
def _lbfgs_fun_grad(x):
   x=np.asarray(x,float)
   sc_lse, sc_ex, gz, c = smooth_z_score_grad(x, CFG['beta'], CFG['n'], CFG['Lc'], CFG['Lh'], CFG['sqrt_n'])
   scale=CFG['scale']
   if not np.isfinite(sc_lse): sc_lse=1e6
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)): gz=np.zeros_like(gz)
   return float(sc_lse)*scale, gz*scale
```

Test for uniform p: n large, beta small q uniform. h_i? Let p=1/n, a=1/√n. q uniform over 2n-1, h_i=(1/(2n-1))∑ a_{k-i}. For middle, ∑_{k=i}^{i+n-1} a=√n? many; h≈√n/(2n)=1/√(2n)? Gp=4√n*1/√(2n)=constant ~√(8/n)? small? Wait if a=1/√n, sum=√n. q uniform (1/(2n)), for k window length ~n, sum a over window ~n/√n=√n, times 1/(2n)=1/(2√n). h=1/(2√n). Gp=4√n*(1/(2√n))=2. C=∑pGp ~? p uniform, avg Gp maybe? Gp edge lower. C maybe 1. gz=p*(Gp-C) ~O(1/n). Good.

Now, effective update in z: for variables with Gp>C, increase log p (mass), for lower decrease. Good.

One more subtlety: The LSE Lagre weights q sum 1. If β small, q broad; objective 2L is not exactly 2M but moment. The gradient ∂L/∂a=2h where h uses q. Yes.

Now, local_search q lse modes: It uses `make_q(c,'lse',20.0)` where q sum 1 over all lags. h=corr(q,a). Direction -h. This is gradient of score with denominator constant? Let's confirm for exact active unique q one-hot: direction -a_{k-i} (projected). Good. For LSE, direction -h. Good.

Now, line search with d projected sum zero. If d has small norm due h nearly constant (e.g., q uniform and sequence symmetric), random directions used.

Potential issue: In local_search, `line_search_a` normalizes d by 10*max_base and searches λ in [-5,5]. That means max change from λ=1 is 10% of max_base. If direction requires small absolute changes because many variables, okay. If exact optimum requires a 50% change in shape, line search over ±5 can get ±50%. Good. If needs tiny, golden can find.

Now, exact search over λ may find boundary where some variables zero. If it zeros a coordinate, line_search clips; sum not exactly zero but close. Future z has p floor; line search can increase if direction positive. Good.

Let's consider the runtime of exact initial for 5 candidates length 10000: 5* c FFT 32768 ~0.1s.

Potential issue with `scipy.optimize.minimize` and global CFG: If two L-BFGS calls are sequential, CFG updated. Good.

Now, final code docstring. Ensure no comments too long? okay.

Let's craft final code in final answer. Need only ```python ...```. Ensure no analysis outside. Let's write carefully.

Double-check syntax for `np.r_[0.0, ynew]`: Need ynew numpy; `np.r_[0.0, ynew]` works. 

`random_direction` lowpass: `freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(L)` returns float. `filt = 1.0 / (1.0 + (freqs / (fc + 1e-300))**2)`.

`local_search` uses `mode = it % 4`, but it increments only inside loop. If `no_improve` triggers random line search after failed direction, and random fails, loop continues; next iteration increments. Good.

`local_search` if line_search returns `sc_new` inf? Compare false. no_improve.

`line_search_a` final candidate evaluation: We compute `sc0` at lam 0. Then golden has fc/fd. After loop, evaluate `mid`, boundaries. But we may also evaluate `c_l`, `d_l` again. The best maybe `a_l` or `b_l`. We'll loop candidates and if not already? duplicate okay. To avoid evaluating boundaries if lo/hi infeasible due clip? okay.

In line_search, after `d = d / (10.0*max_base)`, if `max_base` small (1e-12), d huge normalized? If base all zeros, max_base=1e-12, maybe; but we won't call with zero. Scale okay.

`score_at_lambda` uses `np.min(x)` which costs O(n). Could use `if (x < 0).any()`. Fine.

Now, ensure all functions defined before use? In Python top-level order matters only at call time. `smooth_z_score_grad` uses `linconv`, `correlation_h` defined before? We'll define helpers first.

Potential global `CFG` not set before `_lbfgs_fun_grad` call? lbfgs sets.

Now, about `minimize` method L-BFGS-B without bounds: It may try to evaluate with x as array; ok. `ftol=1e-18` maybe too strict; use 1e-12.

Now, final code. Let's proceed.
</think>

```python
"""
Hybrid nonnegative step-function optimizer.

Strategy:
- Work on the simplex via log-parameterization z; output scale is sqrt(n).
- Use exact projected-direction line searches (subgradient / soft-peak gradients
  and random smooth directions), because exact accepted steps are robust near
  the target precision.
- Use log-sum-exp smoothed objective in z-space with Adam-type updates for
  faster basin exploration and basin shaping.
- Polish with L-BFGS-B on the smoothed objective.
- Multi-start from parent, binomial-square-root shape, and smooth perturbations.
- If time remains, try a longer sequence by interpolating the best sequence.

The returned sequence has nonnegative finite values and is verified once with
evaluate_sequence() when available.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

# Global configuration for the top-level L-BFGS objective (avoids closures).
CFG = {
    "n": 1,
    "Lc": 1,
    "Lh": 1,
    "sqrt_n": 1.0,
    "beta": 1.0,
    "scale": 1.0,
}


def score_key(item):
    return item[0]


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << int((m - 1).bit_length())


def linconv(a, L):
    """Linear self-convolution of a via a given FFT length L."""
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:2 * len(a) - 1]


def exact_score_fast(a, n, Lc):
    """Exact score for a fixed length / FFT length."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    c = linconv(a, Lc)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if M < 0.0:
        M = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * M / (S * S))


def exact_score(a):
    """Exact score for arbitrary sequence length."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    Lc = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    return exact_score_fast(a, n, Lc)


def correlation_h(q, a, Lh):
    """
    h_i = sum_k q_k a_{k-i}, where q has length 2n-1 and a has length n.
    """
    n = len(a)
    RA = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], Lh)
    PQ = np.fft.rfft(q, Lh)
    return np.fft.irfft(RA * PQ, Lh)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def make_q(c, mode, beta):
    """Build lag weights q from observation c and a mode."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = len(c)
    q = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if m else 0.0
    if M < 0.0:
        M = 0.0

    if mode == "top1":
        q[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return q
    if mode == "top5":
        k = min(5, m)
        idx = np.argpartition(c, m - k)[-k:]
        q[idx] = 1.0 / k
        return q
    if mode == "top10":
        k = min(10, m)
        idx = np.argpartition(c, m - k)[-k:]
        q[idx] = 1.0 / k
        return q

    b = float(max(1.0, beta))
    y = np.clip(b * (c - M), -700.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s > 1e-300:
        q = e / s
    else:
        q[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    return q


def scale_to_sqrt(a, n):
    """Rescale a so that sum(a) = sqrt(n)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size != n:
        a = resize_seq_to_len(a, n)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * (np.sqrt(n) / n)
    return a * (np.sqrt(n) / S)


def resize_seq_to_len(a, m):
    """Cumulative-interpolation resize of a nonnegative sequence."""
    m = int(m)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if m <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if S < 1e-12 or a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, a.size - 1.0, a.size)
    y = np.cumsum(a)
    y = y / y[-1]

    xnew = np.linspace(0.0, m - 1.0, m)
    ynew = np.interp(xnew, x, y)
    out = np.diff(np.r_[0.0, ynew])
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, None)
    T = float(out.sum())
    if T < 1e-12:
        out = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) * S / m
    else:
        out = out * (S / T)
    return out


def softmax_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.ones_like(z, dtype=np.float64) / float(z.size)
    else:
        p = p / s
    return p


def seq_to_z(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        a = np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
        S = float(a.sum())
    p = a / S
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    p = p / float(p.sum())
    z = np.log(p)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    return z


def z_to_a(z, sqrt_n):
    return float(sqrt_n) * softmax_z(z)


def z_score(z, n, Lc, sqrt_n):
    p = softmax_z(z)
    a = float(sqrt_n) * p
    return exact_score_fast(a, n, Lc)


def smooth_z_score_grad(z, beta, n, Lc, Lh, sqrt_n):
    """
    Smoothed log-sum-exp objective and gradient w.r.t. z for
    p = softmax(z), a = sqrt_n * p, score approx = 2 * LSE(c).
    Returns (lse_score, exact_score, grad_z, c).
    """
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        p = np.ones_like(z, dtype=np.float64) / float(z.size)
    else:
        p = p / s

    a = float(sqrt_n) * p
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, Lc)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, Lc)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)

    M = float(np.max(c))
    if M < 0.0:
        M = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        M = 0.0
        c = np.zeros_like(c)

    b = float(max(1.0, beta))
    y = np.clip(b * (c - M), -700.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(y)
    es = float(e.sum())
    if es < 1e-300:
        q = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        q[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        L = M
    else:
        q = e / es
        L = M + float(np.log(es)) / b

    h = correlation_h(q, a, Lh)
    h = np.where(h > 0.0, h, 0.0)

    # For p=sum 1, a=√n p, score=2L.  dL/dp = 2√n h.
    Gp = 4.0 * float(sqrt_n) * h
    C = float(np.dot(p, Gp))
    gz = p * (Gp - C)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)):
        gz = np.zeros_like(gz)

    lse_score = 2.0 * L
    exact_score_val = 2.0 * M
    return lse_score, exact_score_val, gz, c


def score_at_lambda(base, d, lam, n, Lc):
    x = base + lam * d
    if float(np.min(x)) < 0.0:
        x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    return exact_score_fast(x, n, Lc)


def line_search_a(base, d, n, Lc, max_evals=20):
    """Golden-section 1D search along a sum-zero direction, with positivity."""
    base = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64)
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    d = d - float(np.mean(d))
    dnrm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
    if dnrm < 1e-14:
        sc = exact_score_fast(base, n, Lc)
        return base.copy(), sc

    max_base = max(float(np.max(base)), 1e-12)
    d = d / (10.0 * max_base)  # λ=1 moves about 10% of max coordinate

    lo = -5.0
    hi = 5.0

    pos = d > 1e-15
    if np.any(pos):
        lim = float(np.max(-base[pos] / d[pos]))  # negative
        lo = max(lo, 0.98 * lim)

    neg = d < -1e-15
    if np.any(neg):
        lim = float(np.max(base[neg] / (-d[neg])))  # positive
        hi = min(hi, 0.98 * lim)

    if lo >= 0.0:
        lo = -1e-7
    if hi <= 0.0:
        hi = 1e-7

    if hi - lo < 1e-10:
        sc = exact_score_fast(base, n, Lc)
        return base.copy(), sc

    best_lam = 0.0
    best_sc = score_at_lambda(base, d, 0.0, n, Lc)

    gr = (np.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0
    if_gr = 1.0 - gr
    a_l = lo
    b_l = hi

    c_l = b_l - if_gr * (b_l - a_l)
    d_l = a_l + if_gr * (b_l - a_l)

    fc = score_at_lambda(base, d, c_l, n, Lc)
    fd = score_at_lambda(base, d, d_l, n, Lc)

    if fc < best_sc:
        best_sc = fc
        best_lam = c_l
    if fd < best_sc:
        best_sc = fd
        best_lam = d_l

    for _ in range(max(0, max_evals - 3)):
        if fc < fd:
            b_l = d_l
            d_l = c_l
            fd = fc
            c_l = b_l - if_gr * (b_l - a_l)
            fc = score_at_lambda(base, d, c_l, n, Lc)
        else:
            a_l = c_l
            c_l = d_l
            fc = fd
            d_l = a_l + if_gr * (b_l - a_l)
            fd = score_at_lambda(base, d, d_l, n, Lc)

        if fc < best_sc:
            best_sc = fc
            best_lam = c_l
        if fd < best_sc:
            best_sc = fd
            best_lam = d_l

        if abs(b_l - a_l) < 1e-9 * max(1.0, abs(c_l), abs(d_l)):
            break

    mid_l = 0.5 * (a_l + b_l)
    fm = score_at_lambda(base, d, mid_l, n, Lc)
    if fm < best_sc:
        best_sc = fm
        best_lam = mid_l

    candidates = (a_l + 1e-9, b_l - 1e-9, c_l, d_l, mid_l, 0.0)
    for lam in candidates:
        if not np.isfinite(lam):
            continue
        if lam < lo - 1e-8 or lam > hi + 1e-8:
            continue
        sc = score_at_lambda(base, d, lam, n, Lc)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_lam = lam

    x = base + best_lam * d
    if float(np.min(x)) < 0.0:
        x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    return x, best_sc


def random_direction(rng, n, kind=0):
    """Random smooth / structured direction with zero mean."""
    n = int(n)
    if kind == 0:
        # Low-pass random Fourier direction.
        L = 1 << int((n - 1).bit_length())
        Lh = L // 2 + 1
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(L)
        fc = float(rng.uniform(1.0 / (20.0 * max(1, n)), 0.25))
        filt = 1.0 / (1.0 + (freqs / max(fc, 1e-300)) ** 2)
        F = (rng.standard_normal(Lh) + 1j * rng.standard_normal(Lh)) * filt
        F[0] = 0.0
        d = np.fft.irfft(F, L)[:n]
    elif kind == 1:
        # Two blocks with opposite signs.
        d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        hi_k = max(2, int(n // 20) + 1)
        if hi_k > n:
            hi_k = n
        k = int(max(1, min(hi_k, rng.integers(1, max(2, hi_k + 1)))))
        if k > n:
            k = max(1, n // 2)
        lim = max(1, n - k + 1)
        i = int(rng.integers(0, lim))
        j = int(rng.integers(0, lim))
        d[i:i + k] = 1.0
        d[j:j + k] = -1.0
    else:
        # Sparse coordinate update.
        d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        hi_c = max(2, int(n // 50) + 1)
        cnt = int(max(1, min(hi_c, rng.integers(1, max(2, hi_c + 1)))))
        inc = rng.integers(0, n, cnt)
        dec = rng.integers(0, n, cnt)
        d[inc] += 1.0
        d[dec] -= 1.0

    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64)
    d -= float(np.mean(d))
    return d


def local_search(a, n, Lc, Lh, time_limit, rng, label=""):
    """Exact greedy line-search local optimization."""
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        s = exact_score_fast(a, n, Lc)
        return np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy(), s

    start = time.monotonic()
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_fast(a, n, Lc)
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() - start < time_limit:
        it += 1
        c = linconv(a, Lc)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        S = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(M) or S < 1e-12:
            a = best_a.copy()
            if no_improve > 5:
                break
            continue

        it_mode = it % 4
        if it_mode == 0:
            q = make_q(c, "top1", 0.0)
        elif it_mode == 1:
            q = make_q(c, "top10", 0.0)
        elif it_mode == 2:
            q = make_q(c, "lse", 20.0)
        else:
            q = make_q(c, "lse", 200.0)

        h = correlation_h(q, a, Lh)
        h = np.where(h > 0.0, h, 0.0)
        d = -h
        d = d - float(np.mean(d))

        a_try, sc_try = line_search_a(a, d, n, Lc, max_evals=18)
        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_sc - 1e-10:
            best_sc = sc_try
            best_a = a_try.copy()
            a = a_try.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve % 2 == 0:
                kind = (no_improve // 2) % 3
                dr = random_direction(rng, n, kind)
                a_rand, sc_rand = line_search_a(a, dr, n, Lc, max_evals=14)
                if np.isfinite(sc_rand) and sc_rand < best_sc - 1e-10:
                    best_sc = sc_rand
                    best_a = a_rand.copy()
                    a = a_rand.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                elif no_improve > 35:
                    break

    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = exact_score_fast(best_a, n, Lc)
    return best_a, best_sc


def adam_optimize_z(z, n, Lc, Lh, sqrt_n, time_limit, label=""):
    """Adam-type optimization in log-simplex z-space with LSE continuation."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        sc = z_score(z, n, Lc, sqrt_n)
        return z, sc, z, sc

    beta_schedule = [
        (10.0, 0.05),
        (30.0, 0.08),
        (100.0, 0.12),
        (300.0, 0.15),
        (1000.0, 0.20),
        (3000.0, 0.25),
        (10000.0, 0.15),
    ]

    start = time.monotonic()
    best_z = z.copy()
    best_exact = float("inf")
    current_z = z.copy()
    current_exact = float("inf")

    m = np.zeros_like(z)
    v = np.zeros_like(z)
    t = 1
    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.995
    eps = 1e-8

    total_frac = sum(fr for _, fr in beta_schedule)
    if total_frac <= 0:
        total_frac = 1.0

    for bi, (beta, frac) in enumerate(beta_schedule):
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        remaining = time_limit - elapsed
        if remaining <= 0.0:
            break
        if bi == len(beta_schedule) - 1:
            sub_time = remaining
        else:
            sub_time = min(remaining, time_limit * frac / total_frac)
        if sub_time <= 0.0:
            continue

        if beta < 30.0:
            lr = 0.08
        elif beta < 100.0:
            lr = 0.07
        elif beta < 300.0:
            lr = 0.06
        elif beta < 1000.0:
            lr = 0.05
        elif beta < 3000.0:
            lr = 0.035
        else:
            lr = 0.02

        beta_start = time.monotonic()
        stall = 0

        while time.monotonic() - beta_start < sub_time and time.monotonic() - start < time_limit:
            lse_sc, exact_sc, gz, c = smooth_z_score_grad(z, beta, n, Lc, Lh, sqrt_n)
            if not np.isfinite(exact_sc) or not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)):
                z = best_z.copy()
                m = 0.5 * m
                v = 0.5 * v
                lr = max(1e-4, 0.5 * lr)
                continue

            current_z = z.copy()
            current_exact = exact_sc

            if exact_sc < best_exact:
                best_exact = exact_sc
                best_z = z.copy()
                stall = 0
            else:
                if exact_sc > best_exact + 0.03:
                    z = best_z.copy()
                    m = 0.2 * m
                    v = 0.5 * v
                    lr = max(1e-4, 0.7 * lr)
                    stall = 0
                else:
                    stall += 1
                    if stall > 8000:
                        lr = max(1e-4, 0.9 * lr)
                        m = 0.5 * m
                        stall = 0

            m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * gz
            v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (gz * gz)
            b1_p = b1 ** t
            b2_p = b2 ** t
            m_hat = m / (1.0 - b1_p)
            v_hat = v / (1.0 - b2_p)
            z = z - lr * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + eps)
            z = z - float(np.mean(z))

            # Keep z range sane to avoid permanent underflow.
            zmin = float(np.min(z))
            zmax = float(np.max(z))
            if np.isfinite(zmin) and np.isfinite(zmax) and (zmax - zmin) > 300.0:
                z = z - zmin - 150.0
                z = z - float(np.mean(z))

            t += 1

        # End-of-beta snapshot.
        lse_sc, exact_sc, gz, c = smooth_z_score_grad(z, beta, n, Lc, Lh, sqrt_n)
        if np.isfinite(exact_sc):
            current_z = z.copy()
            current_exact = exact_sc
            if exact_sc < best_exact:
                best_exact = exact_sc
                best_z = z.copy()
            elif exact_sc > best_exact + 0.05:
                z = best_z.copy()

    # Final snapshot.
    lse_sc, exact_sc, gz, c = smooth_z_score_grad(z, 10000.0, n, Lc, Lh, sqrt_n)
    if np.isfinite(exact_sc):
        current_z = z.copy()
        current_exact = exact_sc
        if exact_sc < best_exact:
            best_exact = exact_sc
            best_z = z.copy()

    if not np.isfinite(best_exact):
        best_exact = z_score(best_z, n, Lc, sqrt_n)
    if not np.isfinite(current_exact):
        current_exact = z_score(current_z, n, Lc, sqrt_n)
        current_z = z.copy()

    return best_z, best_exact, current_z, current_exact


def _lbfgs_fun_grad(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    lse_sc, exact_sc, gz, c = smooth_z_score_grad(
        x, CFG["beta"], CFG["n"], CFG["Lc"], CFG["Lh"], CFG["sqrt_n"]
    )
    if not np.isfinite(lse_sc):
        lse_sc = 1e6
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)):
        gz = np.zeros_like(gz)
    sc = float(lse_sc) * float(CFG["scale"])
    g = gz * float(CFG["scale"])
    return sc, g


def lbfgs_polish(z, n, Lc, Lh, sqrt_n, beta, time_limit):
    """Short L-BFGS-B polish on the LSE objective in z-space."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    sc0 = z_score(z, n, Lc, sqrt_n)
    best_z = z.copy()
    best_sc = sc0

    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return best_z, best_sc

    CFG["n"] = n
    CFG["Lc"] = Lc
    CFG["Lh"] = Lh
    CFG["sqrt_n"] = sqrt_n
    CFG["beta"] = float(beta)
    CFG["scale"] = max(1.0, float(n) / 10.0)

    maxfun = int(min(5000, max(250, time_limit * 10.0)))
    maxiter = int(max(120, maxfun // 5))

    try:
        res = minimize(
            _lbfgs_fun_grad,
            z,
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options={
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": maxfun,
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "maxls": 20,
            },
        )
        z_new = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if np.all(np.isfinite(z_new)):
            z_new = z_new - float(np.mean(z_new))
            sc_new = z_score(z_new, n, Lc, sqrt_n)
            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_new
                best_z = z_new
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best_z, best_sc


def binomial_sqrt(n):
    """Coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), truncated: b_k ~ k^(-1/2)."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    out[0] = 1.0
    if n > 1:
        k = np.arange(1, n, dtype=np.float64)
        out[1:] = np.cumprod((2.0 * k - 1.0) / (2.0 * k))
    return out


def improve_start(a_in, n, Lc, Lh, sqrt_n, time_limit, rng, label=""):
    """Improve one starting sequence within an allotted time."""
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        a = scale_to_sqrt(a_in, n)
        s = exact_score_fast(a, n, Lc)
        return a, s

    start = time.monotonic()
    a = scale_to_sqrt(a_in, n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_fast(a, n, Lc)

    # Stage 1: exact local line search.
    t1 = min(0.10 * time_limit, max(2.0, time_limit - 25.0))
    if t1 > 1.0:
        a1, sc1 = local_search(a, n, Lc, Lh, t1, rng, label + " local1")
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc1
            best_a = a1.copy()
        work_a = a1 if (np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 <= best_sc + 0.02) else best_a
    else:
        work_a = best_a

    # Stage 2: Adam LSE continuation.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
    remaining = time_limit - elapsed
    t2 = min(0.55 * remaining, 0.55 * time_limit)
    if t2 > 10.0:
        z_work = seq_to_z(work_a)
        best_z, sc_z, current_z, sc_cur = adam_optimize_z(
            z_work, n, Lc, Lh, sqrt_n, t2, label + " adam"
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc_z) and sc_z < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_z
            best_a = z_to_a(best_z, sqrt_n)
        if np.isfinite(sc_cur):
            work_z = current_z if sc_cur <= best_sc + 0.02 else best_z
            work_a = z_to_a(work_z, sqrt_n)
        else:
            work_z = best_z
            work_a = z_to_a(work_z, sqrt_n)
    else:
        work_z = seq_to_z(work_a)
        work_a = work_z and work_a

    # Stage 3: exact local line search again from the working point.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
    remaining = time_limit - elapsed
    t3 = min(0.25 * remaining, 0.22 * time_limit)
    if t3 > 2.0 and np.all(np.isfinite(work_a)):
        a3, sc3 = local_search(work_a, n, Lc, Lh, t3, rng, label + " local2")
        if np.isfinite(sc3) and sc3 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc3
            best_a = a3.copy()

    # Stage 4: L-BFGS polish.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
    remaining = time_limit - elapsed
    t4 = max(0.0, remaining - 1.0)
    if t4 > 12.0:
        z_best = seq_to_z(best_a)
        t4a = 0.5 * t4
        t4b = 0.5 * t4

        z_pol, sc_pol = lbfgs_polish(z_best, n, Lc, Lh, sqrt_n, 2000.0, t4a)
        if np.isfinite(sc_pol) and sc_pol < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_pol
            best_a = z_to_a(z_pol, sqrt_n)
            z_best = seq_to_z(best_a)

        # A second polish from the working basin, if it is not essentially best.
        if t4b > 8.0:
            z_work = seq_to_z(work_a)
            if float(np.max(np.abs(z_work - z_best))) > 1e-6:
                z_pol2, sc_pol2 = lbfgs_polish(z_work, n, Lc, Lh, sqrt_n, 5000.0, t4b)
                if np.isfinite(sc_pol2) and sc_pol2 < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc_pol2
                    best_a = z_to_a(z_pol2, sqrt_n)

    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = exact_score_fast(best_a, n, Lc)
    return best_a, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing evaluate_sequence(sequence).
    Must be defined with this signature; uses top-level helpers only.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + total_budget - 25.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    # Load optional parent sequence.
    parent = None
    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals() and globals()["height_sequence_1"] is not None:
            parent = np.asarray(globals()["height_sequence_1"], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            parent = np.where(np.isfinite(parent), parent, 0.0)
            parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if parent.size < 256 or float(parent.sum()) <= 1e-12:
                parent = None
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Primary length.
    if parent is not None:
        if len(parent) >= 2048:
            N = int(min(max(len(parent), 2048), 12000))
        else:
            N = 8192
    else:
        N = 8192

    N = int(min(N, 12288))
    N = int(max(N, 1024))

    Lc_N = next_pow2(2 * N - 1)
    Lh_N = next_pow2(3 * N - 2)
    sqrt_N = float(np.sqrt(N))

    best_a = None
    best_n = N
    best_sc = float("inf")

    def _set_best(a, n, sc):
        nonlocal best_a, best_n, best_sc
        if a is None or n <= 0 or not np.isfinite(sc) or sc >= best_sc:
            return
        aa = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        aa = np.where(np.isfinite(aa), aa, 0.0)
        aa = np.clip(aa, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if len(aa) != n:
            aa = resize_seq_to_len(aa, n)
            aa = np.clip(aa, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if aa.sum() < 1e-12:
            return
        best_a = aa
        best_n = int(n)
        best_sc = float(sc)

    # Build candidate pool.
    cands = []

    if parent is not None:
        if len(parent) == N:
            par = parent.astype(np.float64).copy()
        else:
            par = resize_seq_to_len(parent, N)
        par = np.clip(par, 0.0, 1000.0)
        cands.append(("parent", par))
        cands.append(("parent_rev", par[::-1].copy()))
        S = float(par.sum())
        if S > 1e-12:
            sym = (par + par[::-1]) / (2.0 * S)
            cands.append(("parent_sym", sym * S))

    b = binomial_sqrt(N)
    cands.append(("binomial", b))

    # Smooth random perturbations of binomial, to create distinct basins.
    r1 = random_direction(rng, N, 0)
    s1 = float(np.std(r1))
    if s1 > 1e-12:
        r1 = 0.30 * r1 / s1
        a1 = b * np.exp(np.clip(r1, -2.0, 2.0))
        cands.append(("random1", a1))

    r2a = random_direction(rng, N, 1)
    r2b = random_direction(rng, N, 2)
    s2 = max(float(np.std(r2a)), 1e-12)
    s3 = max(float(np.std(r2b)), 1e-12)
    r2 = 0.20 * r2a / s2 + 0.15 * r2b / s3
    a2 = b * np.exp(np.clip(r2, -2.0, 2.0))
    cands.append(("random2", a2))

    # Evaluate candidates (exact score is scale-invariant).
    scored = []
    for label, a in cands:
        if label.startswith("random") and time.monotonic() > deadline - 300.0:
            continue
        try:
            sc = exact_score_score = exact_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((sc, label, np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not scored:
        b = binomial_sqrt(N)
        scored = [(exact_score(b), "binomial", b)]

    scored.sort(key=score_key)

    # Select at most 3 starts: best, binomial, random if allowed.
    max_starts = 3 if total_budget > 1500.0 else 2
    selected = []
    seen_labels = set()

    chosen_labels_priority = []
    for sc, label, a in scored:
        chosen_labels_priority.append((label, a))

    for sc, label, a in scored:
        if len(selected) >= max_starts:
            break
        if label in seen_labels:
            continue
        selected.append((label, a))
        seen_labels.add(label)

    # Force binomial if present and not selected; it is a very different basin.
    if len(selected) < max_starts:
        for sc, label, a in scored:
            if label == "binomial" and "binomial" not in seen_labels:
                selected.append((label, a))
                seen_labels.add("binomial")
                break

    # Add a random start if present and not selected.
    if len(selected) < max_starts and total_budget > 900.0:
        for sc, label, a in scored:
            if label.startswith("random") and label not in seen_labels:
                selected.append((label, a))
                seen_labels.add(label)
                break

    # Fallback fill with remaining candidates by score.
    if len(selected) < max_starts:
        for sc, label, a in scored:
            if len(selected) >= max_starts:
                break
            if label in seen_labels:
                continue
            selected.append((label, a))
            seen_labels.add(label)

    if not selected:
        b = binomial_sqrt(N)
        selected = [("binomial", b)]

    # Record initial best candidates.
    for label, a in selected:
        try:
            sc = exact_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                _set_best(a, N, sc)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_a is None:
        b = binomial_sqrt(N)
        _set_best(b, N, exact_score(b))

    print(f"Primary length N={N}; best initial={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Main multi-start optimization.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - t_start - elapsed)
    main_budget = 0.62 * remaining
    large_reserved = 0.20 * remaining
    final_reserved = 0.18 * remaining
    if large_reserved > 120.0:
        main_budget += (large_reserved - 120.0) * 0.25
    main_budget = max(30.0, min(main_budget, remaining - final_reserved - 10.0))

    weights = []
    if len(selected) == 1:
        weights = [1.0]
    elif len(selected) == 2:
        weights = [0.62, 0.38]
    else:
        weights = [0.48, 0.30, 0.22]

    main_elapsed = 0.0
    for i, (label, a0) in enumerate(selected):
        t_i = weights[i] * main_budget
        if i == len(selected) - 1:
            t_i = max(0.0, main_budget - main_elapsed)
        t_i = min(t_i, deadline - t_start - (time.monotonic() - t_start) - 60.0)
        if t_i <= 0.0:
            break

        print(f"Main start {i}: {label}, time={t_i:.0f}s", flush=True)
        try:
            a_out, sc_out = improve_start(
                a0, N, Lc_N, Lh_N, sqrt_N, t_i, rng, label
            )
            main_elapsed += time.monotonic() - t_start - main_elapsed
            if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_sc:
                best_a = np.asarray(a_out, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                best_n = N
                best_sc = float(sc_out)
                print(f"  main start {label} improved to {best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)
            elif a_out is not None:
                # Even if not global best, keep for potential further use.
                pass
        except Exception:
            pass

        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 80.0:
            break

    # If nothing finite, fallback to binomial.
    if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        b = binomial_sqrt(N)
        best_a = b
        best_n = N
        best_sc = exact_score(b)

    # Optional larger length stage: interpolate best sequence and optimize.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - t_start - elapsed)
    large_budget = min(remaining, 0.25 * (deadline - t_start))
    if large_budget > 180.0:
        if best_n < 10000:
            N2 = int(min(12288, max(9216, int(1.35 * best_n))))
        else:
            N2 = int(min(12288, best_n + 1200))

        if N2 > best_n:
            Lc_2 = next_pow2(2 * N2 - 1)
            Lh_2 = next_pow2(3 * N2 - 1)
            sqrt_2 = float(np.sqrt(N2))
            a_large = resize_seq_to_len(best_a, N2)
            sc_large0 = exact_score(a_large)
            if np.isfinite(sc_large0):
                _set_best(a_large, N2, sc_large0)

            t_large = max(0.0, large_budget - 20.0)
            if t_large > 120.0:
                print(f"Large grid N2={N2}, time={t_large:.0f}s", flush=True)
                try:
                    a_out, sc_out = improve_start(
                        a_large, N2, Lc_2, Lh_2, sqrt_2, t_large, rng, "large"
                    )
                    if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_sc:
                        best_a = np.asarray(a_out, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                        best_n = N2
                        best_sc = float(sc_out)
                        print(f"  large improved to {best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # Final polish on the global best.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    remaining = max(0.0, deadline - t_start - elapsed)
    t_final = min(remaining, 120.0)
    if t_final > 10.0 and best_a is not None and best_n > 0:
        Lc_f = next_pow2(2 * best_n - 1)
        Lh_f = next_pow2(3 * best_n - 2)
        sqrt_f = float(np.sqrt(best_n))
        try:
            a_final, sc_final = local_search(
                best_a, best_n, Lc_f, Lh_f, t_final, rng, "final"
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc_final) and sc_final < best_sc:
                best_a = np.asarray(a_final, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                best_sc = float(sc_final)
                print(f"Final polish improved to {best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

        # A tiny L-BFGS booster if still enough time.
        remaining2 = max(0.0, deadline - t_start - (time.monotonic() - t_start))
        if remaining2 > 25.0:
            z_final = seq_to_z(best_a)
            try:
                z_pol, sc_pol = lbfgs_polish(z_final, best_n, Lc_f, Lh_f, sqrt_f, 3000.0, remaining2)
                if np.isfinite(sc_pol) and sc_pol < best_sc:
                    best_a = z_to_a(z_pol, sqrt_f)
                    best_sc = float(sc_pol)
                    print(f"Final L-BFGS improved to {best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Construct final list and validate.
    out_arr = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel() if best_a is not None else np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
    out_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(out_arr), out_arr, 0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if out_arr.size == 0 or float(out_arr.sum()) < 1e-6:
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5

    out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]

    # Overdue safety: if we are close to budget, return immediately after one check.
    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    if elapsed > total_budget - 60.0:
        print(f"FINAL local best={best_sc:.8f}, length={len(out)}, time={elapsed:.1f}s", flush=True)
        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            verified = float("inf")
        print(f"FINAL verified={verified:.8f}", flush=True)
        return out

    # Verify with the official evaluator if available.
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(verified) or verified >= 2.0:
        # Very defensive fallback: return binomial shape, which is valid and decent.
        b = binomial_sqrt(max(1024, best_n if best_n else 8192))
        out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, v))) for v in b]
        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            verified = float("inf")

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print("=" * 70, flush=True)
    print(f"FINAL: best_sc={best_sc:.9f}, verified={verified:.9f}", flush=True)
    print(f"length={len(out)}, elapsed={elapsed:.1f}s / {budget_s:.0f}s", flush=True)
    print("=" * 70, flush=True)
    return out
```
